4	the suite SINGLE-PROCESS (pytest default; no xdist installed = no parallel workers). Logs free RAM before
5	the run and every 60 s during it. ABORTS the run (kills the pytest tree) if free RAM falls below the sweep's
6	light floor (0.5 GB) or if the 04:45 hard stop is reached; an abort is a FAIL (HELD day). Writes a verdict
7	JSON so the parent session can proceed to the proof only on a genuine green run.
8	"""
9	import json, os, re, subprocess, sys, time
10	from datetime import datetime, timezone, timedelta
11	
12	REPO = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
13	sys.path.insert(0, REPO)
14	from scripts import memory_guard as M
15	
16	IST = timezone(timedelta(hours=5, minutes=30))
17	HEAVY = M.HEAVY_START_GB          # 2.0 GB — the floor RAM should reach by 03:00
18	LIGHT = M.WEEKEND_SWEEP_FLOOR_GB  # 0.5 GB — the sweep's light floor; abort below it
19	FLOOR = M.LOW_FREE_GB             # NB46: the recorder/session floor (1.2 GB, cited not typed) — below this at
20	#                                   fire time the suite is SKIPPED with one ⏱ line, never started into a reap.
21	RUN_AT = (3, 0)                   # 03:00 IST fallback trigger
22	HARD_STOP = (4, 45)               # 04:45 IST hard stop
23	RAM_LOG = os.path.join(REPO, "reports", "CONFORM-SUITE-RAM.log")
24	RESULT = os.path.join(REPO, "reports", "CONFORM-SUITE-RESULT.json")
25	
26	
27	def skip_if_low(free, floor=None, notify_fn=None):
28	    """NB46 item 3: at fire time, if free RAM is below the floor, the suite must NOT start — it posts ONE ⏱
29	    line with the measured number and skips (never crash-loops). Pure/injectable: returns True if it skipped.
30	    Fail-CLOSED: an unreadable free (None) also skips (the box in which the least is known)."""
31	    floor = FLOOR if floor is None else floor
32	    if free is not None and free >= floor:
33	        return False
34	    line = ("⏱ [E1] Overnight suite SKIPPED — free RAM %s GB is below the %.2f GB floor; not started (no "
35	            "crash-loop). NB44/NB45-A stay BUILT-unverified until a run on a box with enough free RAM."
36	            % ("%.2f" % free if free is not None else "unreadable", floor))
37	    if notify_fn:
38	        notify_fn(line)
39	    _log("SKIP (low RAM): free %s GB < floor %.2f GB — suite not started" %
40	         ("%.2f" % free if free is not None else "unreadable", floor))
41	    return True
42	
43	
44	def _ist():
45	    return datetime.now(IST)
46	
47	
48	def _total_gb():
49	    """Total physical RAM in GB (for peak-usage = total - min free). None if unreadable."""
50	    try:
51	        import ctypes
52	
53	        class _MS(ctypes.Structure):
54	            _fields_ = [("dwLength", ctypes.c_ulong), ("dwMemoryLoad", ctypes.c_ulong),
55	                        ("ullTotalPhys", ctypes.c_ulonglong), ("ullAvailPhys", ctypes.c_ulonglong),
56	                        ("ullTotalPageFile", ctypes.c_ulonglong), ("ullAvailPageFile", ctypes.c_ulonglong),
57	                        ("ullTotalVirtual", ctypes.c_ulonglong), ("ullAvailVirtual", ctypes.c_ulonglong),
58	                        ("ullAvailExtendedVirtual", ctypes.c_ulonglong)]
59	        ms = _MS(); ms.dwLength = ctypes.sizeof(_MS)
60	        if ctypes.windll.kernel32.GlobalMemoryStatusEx(ctypes.byref(ms)):
61	            return round(ms.ullTotalPhys / (1024 ** 3), 2)
62	    except Exception:
63	        pass
64	    return None
65	
66	
67	def _hm(now=None):
68	    now = now or _ist()
69	    return (now.hour, now.minute)
70	
71	
72	def _log(msg):
73	    line = "%s | free %s GB | %s" % (_ist().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"), M.free_gb(), msg)
74	    try:
75	        with open(RAM_LOG, "a", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
76	            fh.write(line + "\n")
77	    except Exception:
78	        pass
79	
80	
81	def _head():
82	    """The git HEAD the suite ran against — pinned into the result so a verify can hash-check it (NB33)."""
83	    try:
84	        return subprocess.run(["git", "rev-parse", "--short", "HEAD"], cwd=REPO,
85	                              capture_output=True, text=True).stdout.strip()
86	    except Exception:
87	        return ""
88	
89	
90	def _write(result):
91	    result["written"] = _ist().isoformat()
92	    result.setdefault("commit", _head())            # NB33: record which HEAD this suite result belongs to
93	    with open(RESULT, "w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
94	        json.dump(result, fh, indent=1)
95	
96	
97	def _wait_for_window():
98	    """Return 'go' when it is time to run, or 'no-window' if we passed the safe start time."""
99	    _log("runner armed; waiting for 03:00 IST or free RAM >= %.1f GB" % HEAVY)
100	    while True:
101	        now = _ist()
102	        f = M.free_gb() or 0.0
103	        if f >= HEAVY:
104	            _log("free RAM reached %.2f GB (>= %.1f) — starting early" % (f, HEAVY))
105	            return "go"
106	        if _hm(now) >= RUN_AT:
107	            _log("03:00 reached, free RAM %.2f GB < %.1f — single-process fallback per ruling" % (f, HEAVY))
108	            return "go"
109	        if _hm(now) >= (4, 20):     # too late to start a ~20-40 min run before the 04:45 hard stop
110	            return "no-window"
111	        time.sleep(60)
112	
113	
114	def _run_suite():
115	    if (M.free_gb() or 0.0) < LIGHT:
116	        _write({"status": "aborted", "why": "free RAM below light floor %.1f GB before start" % LIGHT,
117	                "min_free_gb": M.free_gb(), "held": True})
118	        return
119	    _log("SUITE START (single-process). free RAM printed above; total %s GB." % _total_gb())
120	    out_path = os.path.join(REPO, "reports", "CONFORM-SUITE-PYTEST.txt")
121	    env = dict(os.environ, PYTHONUTF8="1", PYTHONIOENCODING="utf-8")
122	    of = open(out_path, "w", encoding="utf-8")
123	    proc = subprocess.Popen([sys.executable, "-m", "pytest", "tests", "-q", "-p", "no:cacheprovider"],
124	                            cwd=REPO, stdout=of, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, env=env)
125	    min_free = M.free_gb() or 0.0
126	    aborted = None
127	    while proc.poll() is None:
128	        time.sleep(60)
129	        f = M.free_gb()
130	        _log("suite running (pid %s)" % proc.pid)
131	        if f is not None:
132	            min_free = min(min_free, f)
133	        if f is not None and f < LIGHT:
134	            aborted = "free RAM %.2f GB < light floor %.1f GB" % (f, LIGHT)
135	        elif _hm() >= HARD_STOP:
136	            aborted = "04:45 hard stop reached mid-run"
137	        if aborted:
138	            _log("ABORT: %s — killing pytest tree pid %s" % (aborted, proc.pid))
139	            subprocess.run(["taskkill", "/PID", str(proc.pid), "/T", "/F"], capture_output=True)
140	            break
141	    of.close()
142	    total = _total_gb()
143	    peak_used = round(total - min_free, 2) if (total is not None) else None
144	    if aborted:
145	        _write({"status": "aborted", "why": aborted, "min_free_gb": min_free,
146	                "total_gb": total, "peak_used_gb": peak_used, "held": True})
147	        _log("ABORTED: min_free=%.2f GB peak_used=%s GB" % (min_free, peak_used))
148	        return
149	    rc = proc.returncode
150	    try:
151	        text = open(out_path, encoding="utf-8", errors="replace").read()
152	    except Exception:
153	        text = ""
154	    m = re.search(r"(\d+) failed,?\s*(\d+) passed", text)
155	    passed = failed = None
156	    if m:
157	        failed, passed = int(m.group(1)), int(m.group(2))
158	    else:
159	        m2 = re.search(r"(\d+) passed", text)
160	        if m2:
161	            passed, failed = int(m2.group(1)), 0
162	    green = (rc == 0 and (failed == 0))
163	    tail = "\n".join(text.strip().splitlines()[-8:])
164	    try:
165	        from scripts import build_beat
166	        if passed is not None:
167	            build_beat.record_tests(passed, (passed or 0) + (failed or 0))
168	    except Exception:
169	        pass
170	    _write({"status": "green" if green else "red", "returncode": rc, "passed": passed, "failed": failed,
171	            "min_free_gb": min_free, "total_gb": total, "peak_used_gb": peak_used,
172	            "held": (not green), "summary_tail": tail})
173	    _log("SUITE DONE: %s (passed=%s failed=%s rc=%s min_free=%.2f GB peak_used=%s GB) — measured floor input: "
174	         "a clean run needs at least %s GB free to stay above the 0.5 GB abort line"
175	         % ("GREEN" if green else "RED", passed, failed, rc, min_free, peak_used, peak_used))
176	
177	
178	def _notify():
179	    try:
180	        from scripts.notify import notify
181	        return notify
182	    except Exception:
183	        return None
184	
185	
186	def main():
187	    _write({"status": "waiting", "held": None})
188	    if os.environ.get("CONFORM_RUN_NOW") == "1":
189	        _log("START NOW per owner ruling (Edge/Chrome/[redacted] Voice ended); skipping the 03:00 wait.")
190	        if skip_if_low(M.free_gb(), notify_fn=_notify()):     # NB46: ⏱ + skip if below the floor, never reap
191	            _write({"status": "skipped-low-ram", "why": "free RAM below %.2f GB floor at fire time" % FLOOR,
192	                    "min_free_gb": M.free_gb(), "held": True})
193	            return
194	        _run_suite()
195	        return
196	    w = _wait_for_window()
197	    if w == "no-window":
198	        _write({"status": "no-window", "why": "passed 04:20 IST without reaching a start; not enough time before 04:45",
199	                "held": True})
200	        _log("NO WINDOW — not started")
201	        return
202	    if skip_if_low(M.free_gb(), notify_fn=_notify()):         # NB46: ⏱ + skip if below the floor at 03:00
203	        _write({"status": "skipped-low-ram", "why": "free RAM below %.2f GB floor at 03:00 fire time" % FLOOR,
204	                "min_free_gb": M.free_gb(), "held": True})
205	        return
206	    _run_suite()

209	def _demo():
210	    posts = []
211	    nf = lambda m: posts.append(m) or True                    # noqa: E731  (stub the send)
212	    # above the floor -> does NOT skip, says nothing
213	    assert skip_if_low(2.0, floor=1.2, notify_fn=nf) is False and posts == []
214	    # below the floor -> skips with ONE ⏱ line naming the measured number
215	    assert skip_if_low(0.29, floor=1.2, notify_fn=nf) is True
216	    assert len(posts) == 1 and posts[0].startswith("⏱") and "0.29" in posts[0] and "\U0001f64b" not in posts[0]
217	    # unreadable free -> fail-closed skip
218	    posts.clear()
219	    assert skip_if_low(None, floor=1.2, notify_fn=nf) is True and "unreadable" in posts[0]
220	    print("conform_suite_runner demo OK: NB46 ⏱ skip-if-low-RAM guard (never crash-loops; fail-closed).")
221

224	    import sys
225	    if "--demo" in sys.argv:
226	        _demo()
227	    else:
228	        main()

tool_result: 1	"""R-VERIFIER v2 (END-TO-END BUNDLE Part 4) — the independent verifier launcher + gate.
2	
3	No build step counts as done until this has RUN it and written PASS with observed evidence to the mirror. The
4	launcher reads the brain ladder from docs/VERIFIER-BRAIN.md each run (so signing into Codex later upgrades it
5	with no code change), runs the highest available rung with docs/VERIFIER-TEMPLATE.md over docs/VERIFY-MAP.md,
6	writes docs/reports/verify-<commit>.md, and posts one line "VERIFY PASS|FAIL <step> <commit>".
7	
8	The brain invocation is injectable (brain_fn) so the launcher's own logic is tested without spending a run;
9	production wires rung (d) to the supervisor's fresh-independent-Claude mechanism. Independence: the config and
10	launch live in E1-DESK outside the repo, so the build session cannot switch the verifier off (Part 4 step 6).

29	def active_rung():
30	    """Read the ladder and return (rung_letter, name, available). Codex(a) only if signed in; (b)/(c) only if
31	    an API key file exists in E1-DESK; else the fallback (d) fresh-Claude supervisor run, always available."""

36	                               timeout=25, creationflags=0x08000000)
37	            out = ((r.stdout or "") + (r.stderr or "")).lower()   # codex prints the status to stderr
38	            if "logged in" in out and "not logged in" not in out:
39	                return "a", "Codex CLI (ChatGPT sign-in)", True
40	    except Exception:
41	        pass

44	        for f in os.listdir(E1DESK):
45	            lf = f.lower()
46	            if lf.endswith(".key") or "api" in lf and lf.endswith(".txt"):
47	                vendor = "OpenAI" if "openai" in lf else "vendor"
48	                return ("b" if vendor == "OpenAI" else "c"), "API key (%s) in E1-DESK" % vendor, True
49	    except Exception:
50	        pass
51	    # (d) fallback — always available
52	    return "d", "fresh independent Claude run (supervisor mechanism)", True

55	def _report_path(commit):
56	    return os.path.join(REPORTS, "verify-%s.md" % commit)

59	def has_pass(commit):
60	    """Gate helper: True iff a verify report for this commit exists and its verdict is PASS."""
61	    try:
62	        txt = open(_report_path(commit), encoding="utf-8").read()
63	    except Exception:
64	        return False
65	    m = re.search(r"(?im)^\s*VERDICT:\s*(PASS|FAIL|UNAVAILABLE)\b", txt)
66	    return bool(m) and m.group(1).upper() == "PASS"

73	    try:

80	def gate_reason(head=None, arm_file=None):
81	    """The auto-continue gate (Part 4 step 5). Returns None to ALLOW the next item, or a reason string that the
82	    hook emits instead of "continue in order". ADVISORY until the owner arms it by placing E1-DESK\\VERIFIER-ARMED
83	    (so it cannot deadlock the loop before the verifier is proven, and lives outside the repo per step 6). Once
84	    armed: allow only if the last commit has a verify-PASS. The always-allowed actions (reads, git commit,
85	    docs/dispatches, journal, clear) are unaffected — the hook only decides whether to auto-advance."""
86	    arm_file = arm_file or ARM_FILE
87	    if not os.path.exists(arm_file):
88	        return None                                    # not armed -> advisory only, never blocks
89	    head = head or _head()
90	    if not head or has_pass(head):
91	        return None
92	    return "verify the last commit (%s) first — no verify-PASS on the mirror" % head

95	def verify(commit, ruling_text="", claimed_step="", brain_fn=None, post_fn=None, suite_ok_fn=None):
96	    """Run the verifier for one commit. brain_fn(inputs)->{'verdict','evidence'} does the actual exam; default
97	    wires to the active rung. Writes docs/reports/verify-<commit>.md and posts one line. Returns the verdict.
98	    NB33: a PASS is downgraded to FAIL unless suite_ok_fn(commit) confirms the pinned suite ran on THIS HEAD."""
99	    rung, name, avail = active_rung()
100	    post_fn = post_fn or _post
101	    suite_ok_fn = suite_ok_fn or suite_matches
102	    if not avail:
103	        _write_report(commit, "UNAVAILABLE", rung, name, claimed_step, "no brain rung available")
104	        post_fn("VERIFY UNAVAILABLE %s %s" % (claimed_step, commit))
105	        return "UNAVAILABLE"
106	    inputs = {"commit": commit, "ruling": ruling_text, "step": claimed_step,
107	              "diff": _diff(commit), "template": _read(os.path.join(REPO, "docs", "VERIFIER-TEMPLATE.md")),
108	              "map": _read(os.path.join(REPO, "docs", "VERIFY-MAP.md"))}
109	    brain_fn = brain_fn or (lambda i: _run_rung(rung, i))
110	    try:
111	        res = brain_fn(inputs) or {}
112	    except Exception as e:
113	        res = {"verdict": "FAIL", "evidence": "verifier brain raised: %r" % e}
114	    verdict = str(res.get("verdict", "FAIL")).upper()
115	    if verdict not in ("PASS", "FAIL"):
116	        verdict = "FAIL"
117	    evidence = res.get("evidence", "")
118	    actual_rung = res.get("rung", rung)         # NB37: the REAL rung the brain used (a if Codex ran, else d fallback)
119	    actual_name = name if actual_rung == rung else "fresh independent Claude run (rung-d fallback, Codex unavailable)"
120	    if verdict == "PASS":                       # NB33 hash-check: never trust a suite that is not this HEAD's
121	        ok, why = suite_ok_fn(commit)
122	        if not ok:
123	            verdict = "FAIL"
124	            evidence = "HASH-CHECK FAIL (NB33): %s\n\n%s" % (why, evidence)
125	    _write_report(commit, verdict, actual_rung, actual_name, claimed_step, evidence)
126	    post_fn("VERIFY %s %s %s" % (verdict, claimed_step, commit))
127	    if verdict == "FAIL":
128	        try:                            # NB21/NB24: a verifier FAIL is a lead mistake — logs a row + posts one 🚩 line
129	            from scripts import lead_mistakes
130	            lead_mistakes.append("step %s reported done at %s" % (claimed_step or "(unspecified)", commit),
131	                                 "independent verifier returned FAIL", notify_fn=post_fn)
132	        except Exception:
133	            pass
134	    return verdict

143	    except Exception:
144	        pass

162	def suite_commit(result_path=None):

164	    import json as _json

171	def suite_matches(commit, result_path=None):
172	    """(ok, why) — NB33: a PASS is valid ONLY if the pinned suite result (a) carries the commit hash of the EXACT
173	    HEAD being verified, and (b) is GREEN. A missing/different hash is the NB31 hole (a stale suite pinned to a
174	    later HEAD); a red suite for this HEAD cannot back a PASS either. Tolerates short/full hash forms."""
175	    import json as _json
176	    try:
177	        d = _json.loads(open(result_path or SUITE_RESULT, encoding="utf-8").read())
178	    except Exception:
179	        return False, "no readable suite result to hash-check"
180	    sc = str(d.get("commit") or "")
181	    c = str(commit or "")
182	    if not sc:
183	        return False, "the pinned suite result carries NO commit hash — cannot confirm it ran on this HEAD (stale/absent)"
184	    if not (sc == c or (c and sc.startswith(c)) or (sc and c.startswith(sc))):
185	        return False, "the pinned suite result is for commit %s, not the HEAD being verified (%s) — stale" % (sc, c)
186	    if str(d.get("status")) != "green":
187	        return False, "the pinned suite for this HEAD is not green (status=%s, %s failed)" % (d.get("status"), d.get("failed"))
188	    return True, "suite result matches HEAD %s and is green" % c
189	
190	
191	def _suite_evidence():
192	    """R-VERIFIER (owner 23 Sep): the pre-run FULL suite result (run ALONE under the RAM gate), with a sha256 so
193	    the report pins exactly which result was trusted. The verifier reads this instead of re-running the ~21-min
194	    suite (which cannot finish inside a single-shot cap). Returns (summary_line_with_hash, raw_json) or ('','')."""
195	    import hashlib
196	    import json as _json
197	    try:
198	        raw = open(SUITE_RESULT, "rb").read()
199	        h = hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()
200	        d = _json.loads(raw.decode("utf-8"))
201	        line = ("pre-run FULL suite (run ALONE under the RAM gate) sha256=%s: status=%s, %s passed, %s failed, "
202	                "min_free=%s GB, peak_used=%s GB" % (h, d.get("status"), d.get("passed"), d.get("failed"),
203	                                                     d.get("min_free_gb"), d.get("peak_used_gb")))
204	        return line, raw.decode("utf-8", "replace")
205	    except Exception:
206	        return "", ""
207	
208	
209	def _codex_blocked(out):
210	    """True when Codex's reply shows the required checks could not EXECUTE (its sandbox cannot run the Python
211	    interpreter) OR Codex itself was rate-limited/unavailable — i.e. not a substantive verdict, so the caller
212	    falls back to rung (d). NB37: a Codex USAGE-LIMIT is not a verification FAIL (it never ran the checks); it
213	    must fall back to rung-d, exactly like §14.3 treats a usage limit as RETRY, not failure."""
214	    o = (out or "").lower()
215	    return any(m in o for m in ("commandnotfoundexception", "not recognized as", "no installed python",
216	                                "could not execute", "could not start", "failed to start", "checks were blocked",
217	                                "checks failed to start", "usage limit", "rate limit", "quota exceeded",
218	                                "too many requests", "429"))
219	
220	
221	# R-CODEX-CONFINE (owner NB7 item 1): Codex may EXECUTE checks, but ONLY inside a separate clone, with
222	# --sandbox workspace-write (never full-auto), never the live repo. If the clone is absent, Codex is NOT run
223	# against the live repo — the caller falls back to rung (d).
224	CLONE = os.path.join(os.path.expanduser("~"), "Desktop", "E1-DESK", "verify-clone")
225	
226	
227	def _run_codex(prompt):
228	    """Rung (a), CONFINED: Codex runs with cwd = the verify-clone and --sandbox workspace-write, so it may run
229	    and write ONLY inside the clone. Returns None (→ rung d) if the clone is absent, so Codex is NEVER run
230	    against the live repo. Never full-auto."""
231	    if not os.path.isfile(CODEX) or not os.path.isdir(os.path.join(CLONE, ".git")):
232	        return None
233	    try:
234	        # prompt via STDIN ('-'): a ~15k-char prompt as a .cmd arg would hit cmd.exe's 8191-char limit.
235	        r = subprocess.run([CODEX, "exec", "--sandbox", "workspace-write", "-"], input=prompt,
236	                           capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=VERIFY_CAP, cwd=CLONE,
237	                           creationflags=0x08000000)
238	        out = (r.stdout or "") + (("\n" + r.stderr) if r.stderr else "")
239	        return out if out.strip() else None
240	    except Exception:
241	        return None
242	
243	
244	def _live_fingerprint(root=None):
245	    """A cheap tamper fingerprint (HEAD + porcelain-status hash) to PROVE the live repo is untouched around a
246	    confined Codex run. None if git cannot be read."""
247	    import hashlib
248	    root = root or REPO
249	    try:
250	        head = subprocess.run(["git", "-C", root, "rev-parse", "HEAD"], capture_output=True, text=True).stdout.strip()
251	        status = subprocess.run(["git", "-C", root, "status", "--porcelain"], capture_output=True, text=True).stdout
252	        return head + ":" + hashlib.sha256(status.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
253	    except Exception:
254	        return None
255	
256	
257	def prepare_clone():
258	    """Create/refresh the LIGHT verify-clone FROM the live repo (a pull; the live repo is only READ, never
259	    written). Drops the heavy .snapshot/site trees the verify does not need. Returns the clone path or None."""
260	    import shutil
261	    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(CLONE), exist_ok=True)
262	    if not os.path.isdir(os.path.join(CLONE, ".git")):
263	        if subprocess.run(["git", "clone", "--local", "--no-hardlinks", "--depth", "1", REPO, CLONE],
264	                          capture_output=True, text=True).returncode != 0:
265	            return None
266	    else:
267	        subprocess.run(["git", "-C", CLONE, "fetch", "--depth", "1", REPO, "HEAD"], capture_output=True, text=True)
268	        subprocess.run(["git", "-C", CLONE, "checkout", "-f", "FETCH_HEAD"], capture_output=True, text=True)
269	    for heavy in (".snapshot", "site"):                  # not needed by the verify; keeps the clone RAM/disk-light
270	        shutil.rmtree(os.path.join(CLONE, heavy), ignore_errors=True)
271	    return CLONE if os.path.isdir(os.path.join(CLONE, ".git")) else None
272	
273	
274	def _run_rung(rung, inputs):
275	    """Production brain. rung (a) = Codex (codex.cmd exec); rung (d) = fresh independent Claude (supervisor
276	    mechanism); (a) falls back to (d) on any Codex failure. Both are handed the pre-run FULL suite result +
277	    its hash and told NOT to re-run the ~21-min suite (it cannot finish in a single shot); they run only the
278	    fast TARGETED checks (module demo + touched-module tests) and confirm the suite hash."""
279	    import sys as _sys
280	    py = _sys.executable                                  # the real interpreter; the 'py' launcher is NOT installed
281	    suite_line, suite_blob = _suite_evidence()
282	    prompt = ("You are the INDEPENDENT VERIFIER. Follow docs/VERIFIER-TEMPLATE.md and run ONLY the TARGETED checks "
283	              "in docs/VERIFY-MAP.md for the touched paths. Run commands with THIS exact Python interpreter (the "
284	              "'py' launcher is NOT installed on this machine): %s\n"
285	              "Targeted checks to run and quote the output of (the -B avoids .pyc writes under a read-only sandbox):\n"
286	              "  1) \"%s\" -B -m engine1_rebuild.conform\n"
287	              "  2) \"%s\" -B -m pytest tests/test_conform.py -q -p no:cacheprovider\n"
288	              "DO NOT re-run the full ~21-min suite: its result was produced ALONE under the RAM gate and is given "
289	              "below as authoritative evidence — confirm the sha256 matches reports/CONFORM-SUITE-RESULT.json on "
290	              "disk. Do NOT modify, stage, or commit any file; run read-only checks only. A claim without a "
291	              "running-evidence line is FAIL. Reply with a first line 'VERDICT: PASS' or 'VERDICT: FAIL' then the "
292	              "evidence.\n\nPRE-RUN SUITE: %s\n%s\n\nRULING:\n%s\n\nCLAIMED STEP: %s\n\nCOMMIT %s DIFF:\n%s\n" %
293	              (py, py, py, suite_line, suite_blob[:1500], inputs["ruling"][:6000], inputs["step"], inputs["commit"],
294	               inputs["diff"]))
295	    out = None
296	    actual_rung = rung
297	    if rung == "a":
298	        prepare_clone()          # NB37 item 13: create/refresh the confined clone so Codex CAN run (was never called)
299	        cx = _run_codex(prompt)
300	        # Trust a Codex PASS, or a Codex FAIL where the checks ACTUALLY RAN. Fall back to rung (d) only when
301	        # Codex produced nothing OR its sandbox could not execute the checks (owner ruling: rung (d) fallback).
302	        if cx is not None and (re.search(r"(?im)^\s*VERDICT:\s*PASS\b", cx) or not _codex_blocked(cx)):
303	            out = cx
304	    if out is None:
305	        actual_rung = "d"        # NB37: Codex absent/blocked -> the REAL brain is rung-d (report the truth)
306	        from scripts import supervisor
307	        out, _usage = supervisor._invoke_claude(prompt, timeout=VERIFY_CAP)   # (text, usage) -> unpack
308	    out = out or ""
309	    verdict = "PASS" if re.search(r"(?im)^\s*VERDICT:\s*PASS\b", out) else "FAIL"
310	    evidence = ((suite_line + "\n\n") if suite_line else "") + out            # pin the suite hash into the report
311	    return {"verdict": verdict, "evidence": evidence[:6000], "rung": actual_rung}

319	def _demo():
320	    import tempfile, os as _os
321	    global REPORTS

327	               brain_fn=lambda i: {"verdict": "PASS", "evidence": "pytest: 10 passed"}, post_fn=posts.append,
328	               suite_ok_fn=lambda c: (True, "ok"))

330	    f = verify("badc0de", claimed_step="2",
331	               brain_fn=lambda i: {"verdict": "FAIL", "evidence": "test_x failed"}, post_fn=posts.append)
332	    # NB31: a FAIL posts the VERIFY line AND a read-only 🚩 lead-mistake line after it

334	    assert any(p.startswith("🚩 LEAD MISTAKE") for p in posts)
335	    rung, name, avail = active_rung()
336	    assert avail and rung in ("a", "b", "c", "d")

864	def poll_loop(poll_secs=120, max_polls=None):
865	    """The session poll used by the post-login chain. Re-checks gate.order_ok on EVERY poll (fail-closed).
866	    Detached, read-only market data, paper only. Exits at the close or after max_polls. Single-instance:
867	    exits immediately if another runner already holds the lock (R-DEDUPE)."""
868	    import time
869	    from engine1_rebuild import scan
870	    if not _take_single_instance_lock():
871	        print("book20_runner: another instance is already running — exiting (single-instance lock).")
872	        return
873	    n = 0
874	    while True:
875	        scan.write_heartbeat("book20", "20-day book poll")
876	        run_once(write=True)
877	        n += 1
878	        if max_polls and n >= max_polls:
879	            break
880	        time.sleep(poll_secs)

304	_CAND_CACHE = {"ts": 0.0, "asof": None, "data": None}
305	CAND_TTL_SECS = 300          # R-ENGINE-ROOM fix: the candidate scan is heavy (~1-2 min, reads every stock's
306	#                              bars); cache it for 5 min so a 2-minute poll is O(gate-check), never a re-scan.
307	#                              The per-poll LIVE check (moment_light on each candidate) still runs every poll.

310	def _candidates(asof=None, reader=None):
311	    """Daily-direction fires today (engine 1's own signal source), each with its walk-forward LB and horizon.
312	    Reuses the evening signal source; returns [] on any shortage (fail-safe). CACHED for CAND_TTL_SECS so a
313	    poll never re-runs the full scan (which was hanging the runner under recorder disk load).
314	
315	    R-MORNING-22 item 1: E1/E10/E11 trade ONLY the R-EXAMS-REAL survivors (the live pattern set). A fire whose
316	    (symbol, family) is not a survivor is recorded to its walk-forward DIARY and NOT returned for trading — no
317	    orders, no cards. Survivor fires carry their chosen management. FAIL-CLOSED: an empty/unreadable live set
318	    means no survivors, so every fire becomes a diary (a set that cannot be verified is never traded)."""
319	    import time as _t
320	    if reader is None and asof == _CAND_CACHE["asof"] and (_t.time() - _CAND_CACHE["ts"]) < CAND_TTL_SECS \
321	            and _CAND_CACHE["data"] is not None:
322	        return _CAND_CACHE["data"]                    # fresh cache -> skip the heavy scan
323	    out = _candidates_scan(asof=asof, reader=reader)
324	    if reader is None:                                # only cache the production (live) path
325	        _CAND_CACHE.update({"ts": _t.time(), "asof": asof, "data": out})
326	    return out

329	def _candidates_scan(asof=None, reader=None):
330	    """The actual (heavy) candidate scan — see _candidates for the contract."""
331	    try:
332	        from engine1_rebuild import evening, render
333	        from scripts import live_pattern_set as LPS
334	        reader = reader or (lambda t: ds.read(t, "raw"))
335	        cand = evening.active_daily_direction_signals()
336	        out = []
337	        for _, r in cand.iterrows():
338	            tok = int(r["stock"]); cond = r.get("condition"); metric = r.get("metric")
339	            if not isinstance(cond, str):
340	                continue
341	            fired, daily = evening._fire_today(reader, tok, metric, cond, asof)
342	            if not fired:
343	                continue
344	            try:
345	                sym = str(render.symbol(tok))
346	            except Exception:
347	                sym = str(tok)
348	            if LPS.in_live_set(sym, metric):           # ONLY the live set (the R-EXAMS-REAL survivors) trades
349	                mgmt = LPS.management_for(sym, metric) or {}
350	                out.append({"stock": tok, "direction": r["direction"], "p_lb": float(r["train_lb"]),
351	                            "sentence": cond, "daily": daily, "metric": metric,
352	                            "management": mgmt.get("management", ""), "horizon_days": mgmt.get("horizon", H)})
353	            # a fire not in the live set is left to its walk-forward diary (maintained by the diary layer);
354	            # book20 simply never trades it — no order, no card.
355	        return out
356	    except Exception:
357	        return []

360	def run_once(asof=None, reader=None, write=True, quotes=None):
361	    """One poll of the single order path. Returns a readout. Writes an order ONLY when gate.order_ok holds
362	    for it on this poll; under the R-HOLD-AND-FIX hold that is never, so this records the would-be book."""
363	    now_iso = clock.iso()
364	    streak = _live_loss_streak()
365	    cost_frac = money.COST_STATUTORY_RT + 2 * money.half_spread()
366	    cands = _candidates(asof, reader)
367	    by_stock = {c["stock"]: c for c in cands}
368	    # desk rules as CALLERS (R-HOLD-AND-FIX 2): net same-stock fires, and pause on the measured book guard.
369	    fired_orders = [{"stock": c["stock"], "bucket": "daily", "direction": c["direction"], "size": 1.0,
370	                     "strength": abs(c["p_lb"]), "sentence": c["sentence"]} for c in cands]
371	    netted = {int(o["stock"]): o for o in desk.net_orders(fired_orders)} if fired_orders else {}
372	    # R-BOOK20-COMPLETE (3): book guard (measured worst daily DD) + position cap (measured), both fail-closed.
373	    open_now = _open_position_count()
374	    open_keys = _open_pattern_keys()                   # R-FIX-3E (1): (instrument, pattern) already holding a position
375	    block_all = ("*", "*") in open_keys                # fail-closed sentinel: unreadable ledger → no new fires
376	    pos_cap = _measured_position_cap()
377	    cap_paused = open_now >= pos_cap
378	    guard_paused, guard_reason = desk.book_guard(_today_loss_rupees(), _measured_worst_daily_dd())
379	    would_be, written = [], []
380	    for stock, c in list(by_stock.items()):
381	        if stock not in netted:                       # netting cancelled this stock's fires -> no order
382	            continue
383	        c["direction"] = "direct" if netted[stock]["direction"] in ("direct", "long") else "inverse"
384	        tm = _typical_move(c["daily"]["close"] if hasattr(c["daily"], "columns") else c["daily"])
385	        if tm is None:
386	            continue
387	        ok, required = live_20d.required_move_ok(c["p_lb"], cost_frac, tm)
388	        if not ok:
389	            continue
390	        # moment light on the read-only quote (fail-closed offline -> not green -> record-only)
391	        inst = f"NSE:{c['stock']}"
392	        if quotes is not None:
393	            m_ok, _note = desk.moment_light(quotes.get(c["stock"]), key=inst)   # A1: per-stock ceiling
394	        else:
395	            m_ok, _note = live_20d.moment_light_live(inst)
396	        row = {"stock": c["stock"], "direction": c["direction"], "sentence": c["sentence"],
397	               "pattern": c.get("sentence", ""),      # R-FIX-3E (1): the open row carries its pattern for the re-fire key
398	               "required_move": round(required, 5), "typical_move": round(tm, 5),
399	               "moment_green": bool(m_ok), "horizon_days": H}
400	        would_be.append(row)
401	        # R-FIX-3E (1): NO RE-FIRE — never open a second position in a stock this pattern already holds.
402	        refire = block_all or (str(c["stock"]).strip(), str(c.get("sentence", "")).strip()) in open_keys
403	        allowed = (_exam_conform_proven() and (not guard_paused) and (not cap_paused) and (not refire)
404	                   and gate.order_ok(engine=1, moment_green=(m_ok is True), live_loss_streak=streak))
405	        # R-DAY1-BUNDLE A5: one card per fire per engine at fire time (PLACED / HELD), once per fire.
406	        _fire_cards(c, c.get("metric", ""), m_ok, bool(write and allowed), refire, cap_paused, guard_paused)
407	        if write and allowed:
408	            # R-GO-LIVE-SIMPLE (1): FILL AT THE TOUCH immediately on authorisation — long@ask / short@bid at the
409	            # size available; the would-be mid fill is ledgered beside it. A fault here is ledgered, no order.
410	            # R-FIX-3E (1): trade id is unique per fire (…-<fire ts>), never overwritten.
411	            tid = "BOOK20-%s-%s" % (c["stock"], now_iso)
412	            side = "buy" if c["direction"] in ("direct", "long", "buy") else "sell"
413	            try:
414	                pos = fill_at_touch(tid, c["stock"], side, (quotes or {}).get(c["stock"]),
415	                                    _atr_of(c), H, sentence=c.get("sentence", ""), cost_frac=cost_frac)
416	                if pos:
417	                    pos["metric"] = c.get("metric", "")   # A4: carry the fire's family to the E10/E11 books
418	                    written.append({**row, **pos})
419	                    _entry_message(pos)               # 📈 on every paper entry
420	            except Exception as e:
421	                _fault(tid, "fill_at_touch", e)       # ledgered fault -> no order
422	    if written:
423	        _append_orders(written)
424	    managed = _manage_open_positions(quotes)          # manage_position called per open position (📉 on exit)
425	    filled_this_poll = written
426	    readout = {"mode": gate.mode(), "held": gate.held(), "orders_allowed_now": gate.order_ok(1, moment_green=True),
427	               "live_loss_streak": streak, "candidates": len(cands), "netted_orders": len(netted),
428	               "filled_this_poll": len(filled_this_poll),
429	               "positions_managed": managed, "open_positions": open_now,

BUILT-not-LIVE. Each is diary-only (no order path), RAM-gated (its footprint calibrated on a first run, §4.1 —
no typed number), quiet (⏱ to its diary, never 🙋), killed-first. They switch on in the after-close window: a
first supervised run records each engine's footprint (diary_engine.record_footprint), after which §27c gates it.

ONE module, not 8 near-identical files (Ponytail: fewest files); each engine is a clearly-separated wrapper in
ENGINES. The per-engine DATA logic (reading each recorder's store) is the after-close per-engine work under its
own rule-vs-live audit; tonight's work_fn is the honest diary scaffold that names its source. The value built now
is the 8 §27-compliant processes + their smoke test — isolation by construction, before any of them run.

    def work():
        # honest scaffold: the §27-gated diary process is built; the per-engine data logic is its after-close item.
        return "%s diary scaffold — awaiting %s (BUILT-not-LIVE)" % (desc, source)
    return diary_engine.DiaryEngine(name, footprint_gb=None, work_fn=work)   # footprint None -> resolves on run


ENGINES = {name: _make(name, desc, source) for (name, desc, source) in _SPECS}


def run_once(name, ready_seen, free_gb, reopen_floor_gb=None):
    """Run one diary cycle for one engine through its §27 gate. Unknown name -> None."""
    eng = ENGINES.get(name)
    return eng.run_once(ready_seen, free_gb, reopen_floor_gb) if eng else None

tool_result: """NB40 item 2 (foundation) — the DIARY-ONLY ENGINE BASE, with CLAUDE.md §27 (b)-(d) gates baked in.

Every non-chain engine runs as its own process THROUGH this base, so §27 isolation is enforced by construction,
not by each engine remembering it:
  (b) DIARY-ONLY — record() writes only the engine's own diary; there is NO order path here, ever.
  (c) STARTS LATE, RAM-GATED — should_start() is True only AFTER the 08:15 READY line AND when measured free RAM
      >= the reopen floor + this engine's OWN measured footprint (both passed in — measured, no typed number, §4.1).
  (d) QUIET — a fault is caught and written to the engine's diary/held log; it NEVER posts a 🙋 (only a ✅/⏱ line).
  killed-first — PRIORITY marks these processes so the watchdog kills them BEFORE the chain (the chain is never
      killed for another engine).

The engine's work is INJECTED (`work_fn`), so the base is unit-tested with a fake and spends nothing; each of the
8 engine items becomes a thin wrapper: `DiaryEngine("E-x", footprint_gb=<measured>, work_fn=...)`. BUILT + tested;
an engine is LIVE only after its OWN rule-vs-live audit + smoke check, switched on in the after-close window.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import datetime as _dt
import os

_REPO = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
DIARY_DIR = os.path.join(_REPO, "reports", "diaries")
PRIORITY = "diary"          # killed-first marker (§27c): the watchdog kills PRIORITY=="diary" processes before the chain


def _reopen_floor():
    try:
        from scripts import session_resurrect
        return session_resurrect.REOPEN_FLOOR_GB
    except Exception:
        return 0.72          # the measured reopen floor (session_resurrect.REOPEN_FLOOR_GB); fallback only


def _footprint_path(name):
    return os.path.join(DIARY_DIR, "%s.footprint" % name)


def measured_footprint(name):
    """This engine's OWN measured RAM footprint in GB, recorded on a first run — or None if not yet calibrated
    (fail-closed: an uncalibrated engine does NOT start; §4.1 no typed footprint)."""
    try:
        return float(open(_footprint_path(name), encoding="utf-8").read().strip())
    except Exception:
        return None


def record_footprint(name, gb=None):
    """Record this engine's measured footprint. gb=None measures THIS process's peak working set now (calibration
    on first run). Returns the GB recorded."""
    if gb is None:
        gb = _peak_ws_gb()
    if gb is None:
        return None
    try:
        os.makedirs(DIARY_DIR, exist_ok=True)
        open(_footprint_path(name), "w", encoding="utf-8").write("%.3f" % float(gb))
    except Exception:
        pass
    return round(float(gb), 3)


def _peak_ws_gb():
    """Peak working set of THIS process in GB (Windows), or None — the measured footprint on a first run."""
    try:
        import ctypes
        from ctypes import wintypes

        class _PMC(ctypes.Structure):
            _fields_ = [("cb", wintypes.DWORD), ("PageFaultCount", wintypes.DWORD),
                        ("PeakWorkingSetSize", ctypes.c_size_t), ("WorkingSetSize", ctypes.c_size_t),
                        ("QuotaPeakPagedPoolUsage", ctypes.c_size_t), ("QuotaPagedPoolUsage", ctypes.c_size_t),
                        ("QuotaPeakNonPagedPoolUsage", ctypes.c_size_t), ("QuotaNonPagedPoolUsage", ctypes.c_size_t),
                        ("PagefileUsage", ctypes.c_size_t), ("PeakPagefileUsage", ctypes.c_size_t)]
        pmc = _PMC(); pmc.cb = ctypes.sizeof(_PMC)
        h = ctypes.windll.kernel32.GetCurrentProcess()
        if ctypes.windll.psapi.GetProcessMemoryInfo(h, ctypes.byref(pmc), pmc.cb):
            return round(pmc.PeakWorkingSetSize / (1024 ** 3), 3)
    except Exception:
        pass
    return None


def should_start(ready_seen, free_gb, footprint_gb, reopen_floor_gb=None):
    """§27c: a non-chain engine starts ONLY after the 08:15 READY line AND with free RAM at/above the reopen floor
    PLUS this engine's own measured footprint. Fail-closed: unknown/None RAM or footprint -> do not start."""
    if not ready_seen:
        return False
    floor = _reopen_floor() if reopen_floor_gb is None else reopen_floor_gb
    if free_gb is None or footprint_gb is None:
        return False
assistant: Calibration measures each engine process's peak working set. Let me see `run_once` and whether there's a supervised-calibration entry point:
tool_use Bash {"command": "sed -n '90,175p' scripts/diary_engine.py", "description": "Read DiaryEngine run_once + calibration"}
tool_result:         return False
    return float(free_gb) >= float(floor) + float(footprint_gb)


class DiaryEngine:
    """A non-chain engine: diary-only, RAM-gated, quiet, killed-first (§27 b-d). Orders are impossible here."""

    priority = PRIORITY

    def __init__(self, name, footprint_gb, work_fn, diary_fn=None, clock_fn=None):
        self.name = name
        self.footprint_gb = footprint_gb          # this engine's OWN measured RAM footprint (§27c)
        self.work_fn = work_fn                     # the engine's diary work; returns a short status string
        self.diary_fn = diary_fn or self._append_diary
        self.clock_fn = clock_fn or (lambda: _dt.datetime.now().strftime("%H:%M:%S"))

    def _append_diary(self, line):
        try:
            os.makedirs(DIARY_DIR, exist_ok=True)
            with open(os.path.join(DIARY_DIR, "%s.log" % self.name), "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                f.write(line + "\n")
        except Exception:
            pass

    def run_once(self, ready_seen, free_gb, reopen_floor_gb=None):
        """Run one diary cycle IF the §27c gate allows. Returns {started, status|reason}. A fault is caught and
        written to the diary as a quiet ⏱ line — NEVER a 🙋 (§27d)."""
        fp = self.footprint_gb if self.footprint_gb is not None else measured_footprint(self.name)
        if not should_start(ready_seen, free_gb, fp, reopen_floor_gb):
            return {"started": False, "reason": "gate: ready=%s free=%s footprint=%s (None=uncalibrated)" % (
                ready_seen, free_gb, fp)}
        try:
            status = self.work_fn()               # diary-only work; no order path exists to reach
            line = "✅ %s %s — %s" % (self.name, self.clock_fn(), status)
        except Exception as e:                     # §27d: quiet — the fault goes to the diary, no owner ping
            line = "⏱ %s %s — fault %s (diary-only, not an owner ping)" % (self.name, self.clock_fn(), type(e).__name__)
            self.diary_fn(line)
            return {"started": True, "status": "fault", "line": line}
        self.diary_fn(line)
        return {"started": True, "status": "ok", "line": line}


def _demo():
    floor = 0.72
    # §27c gate: needs ready + free RAM >= floor + footprint
    assert should_start(True, floor + 0.3, 0.2, floor) is True          # 1.02 >= 0.72+0.2
    assert should_start(True, floor + 0.1, 0.2, floor) is False         # 0.82 < 0.92 -> hold
    assert should_start(False, 9.9, 0.2, floor) is False                # not ready -> hold (before 08:15)
    assert should_start(True, None, 0.2, floor) is False                # unknown RAM -> fail-closed
    posts = []
    eng = DiaryEngine("E-demo", footprint_gb=0.2, work_fn=lambda: "12 evaluated, 3 fired", diary_fn=posts.append,
                      clock_fn=lambda: "10:00:00")
    # gated off before READY
    assert eng.run_once(False, 9.9)["started"] is False and posts == []
    # runs after READY with RAM -> one ✅ diary line, no ping
    r = eng.run_once(True, floor + 0.5)
    assert r["started"] and r["status"] == "ok" and posts[-1].startswith("✅ E-demo") and "fired" in posts[-1]
    # a faulting engine stays quiet: a ⏱ diary line, never a 🙋
    bad = DiaryEngine("E-bad", 0.1, work_fn=lambda: (_ for _ in ()).throw(RuntimeError("boom")), diary_fn=posts.append,
                      clock_fn=lambda: "10:01:00")
    rb = bad.run_once(True, floor + 0.5)
    assert rb["status"] == "fault" and posts[-1].startswith("⏱ E-bad") and "🙋" not in posts[-1]
    print("diary_engine demo OK: §27c RAM+READY gate; diary-only ✅ line; faults quiet (⏱, never 🙋); killed-first marker.")

tool_result: 59	def evaluate_at_close(asof=None, reader=None, quotes=None):
60	    """For each live pattern, decide at the completed daily bar `asof`. Returns a list of decisions:
61	        {stock, family, fired: bool, verdict: 'QUEUE'|'HELD'|'NO-FIRE', reason, management, check}
62	    Reuses evening._fire_today (signal) and LB.checklist (the six checks). No order is written here."""
63	    from engine1_rebuild import evening
64	    from scripts import liquidity_backtest as LB
65	    reader = reader or (lambda t: ds.read(int(t), "raw"))
66	    k, k_meas = _impact_k(LB)
67	    decisions = []
68	    for p in live_patterns():
69	        mgmt = {"horizon": p["horizon"], "stop_atr": p["stop_atr"], "trail": p["trail"]}
70	        c = p["candidate"]
71	        if not c:
72	            decisions.append(_d(p, False, "HELD", "warm-up — no live signal source for this pattern yet",
73	                                mgmt, "warm-up"))
74	            continue
75	        try:
76	            fired, daily = evening._fire_today(reader, c["token"], c["metric"], c["condition"], asof=asof)
77	        except Exception as e:
78	            decisions.append(_d(p, False, "HELD", "warm-up — signal not computable (%s)" % e, mgmt, "warm-up"))
79	            continue
80	        if not fired:
81	            decisions.append(_d(p, False, "NO-FIRE", "pattern did not fire at the close", mgmt, ""))
82	            continue
83	        # it fired at the close -> run the SAME six checks the exam gates fills with, on the exit-window move
84	        entry = float(daily["close"].values[-1])
85	        bar_date = str(daily.index[-1])[:10]                # the signal's own bar date (proxy/depth basis)
86	        chk = _checklist(LB, c["token"], entry, quotes, bar_date)
87	        if chk.get("verdict") == "HELD":
88	            decisions.append(_d(p, True, "HELD", chk.get("reason", "a check failed"), mgmt,
89	                                _named_check(chk.get("reason", ""))))
90	        else:
91	            decisions.append(_d(p, True, "QUEUE", "fires + six checks pass — queue next-open order", mgmt, ""))
92	    return decisions

100	def queue_orders(asof=None, reader=None, quotes=None, write=True):
101	    """Evaluate at the close and, for each QUEUE decision, write a next-open (EOD-) order that the EXISTING
102	    tested path (evening.process_eod_positions -> paper.simulate_trade at the next open) fills with the exam's
103	    own management (template EXAM_TEMPLATE + the pattern's horizon; manage=trail — all five are trail=false).
104	    HELD signals are logged with the named check, never as a trade. Returns {queued, held, decisions}.
105	
106	    GATED on the R-EXAM-CONFORM proof marker: no order is written until conformance is proven (the fail-safe)."""
107	    from engine1_rebuild import book20_runner as B, paper
108	    decisions = evaluate_at_close(asof=asof, reader=reader, quotes=quotes)
109	    proven = os.path.exists(B.EXAM_CONFORM_PROOF)
110	    asof_date = str(asof)[:10] if asof is not None else _today_str()
111	    rows, held = [], []
112	    for d in decisions:
113	        if d["verdict"] == "QUEUE" and proven:
114	            p = next((x for x in live_patterns() if x["stock"] == d["stock"] and x["family"] == d["family"]), None)
115	            if not p or not p.get("candidate"):
116	                continue
117	            hz = p["horizon"]
118	            rows.append({
119	                "trade_id": "EOD-CONFORM-%s-%s" % (d["stock"], asof_date), "status": "queued",

from scripts import diary_engines as DE


def test_all_eight_engines_present():
    assert len(DE.ENGINES) == 8
    assert set(DE.names()) == {"Overnight", "Insurance", "Pairs", "YouTube", "Calendar", "Dubai",
                               "MachineFactory", "Context"}


def test_each_is_diary_only_and_fail_closed_uncalibrated():
    for name, eng in DE.ENGINES.items():
        assert eng.priority == "diary"                                # killed-first marker (§27c)
        assert not any(hasattr(eng, m) for m in ("place", "order", "buy", "sell")), name   # no order path (§27b)
        assert DE.run_once(name, True, 9.9, 0.72)["started"] is False, name   # uncalibrated -> does not start


def test_calibrated_engine_records_a_quiet_diary_line():
    e = DE.ENGINES["Insurance"]
    posts = []
    e.diary_fn = posts.append
    e.footprint_gb = 0.2                                              # simulate a recorded footprint
    try:
        r = e.run_once(True, 1.5, 0.72)
        assert r["started"] and r["status"] == "ok"
        assert posts[-1].startswith("✅ Insurance") and "🙋" not in posts[-1]
    finally:
        e.footprint_gb = None


def test_demo():
    DE._demo()

def _demo():
    assert len(ENGINES) == 8, names()
    floor = 0.72
    for name, eng in ENGINES.items():
        # (1) no order path: a DiaryEngine has no place/order method — diary-only by construction
        assert not any(hasattr(eng, m) for m in ("place", "order", "buy", "sell")), name
        # (2) fail-closed while UNCALIBRATED (footprint None) — BUILT-not-LIVE

516	def _recorder_ram_ok():
517	    """§14.10 amendment gate: free RAM above the session floor memory_guard.LOW_FREE_GB (cited, not typed).
518	    Recorders are essentials, so this is the low session floor, not the 2 GB heavy gate."""
519	    try:
520	        from scripts import memory_guard as MG
521	        f = MG.free_gb()
522	        return f is not None and f > MG.LOW_FREE_GB
523	    except Exception:
524	        return False                                       # fail-closed

528	#                                  not every 5-min cycle (no timer-repeating message — NB21). The RELAUNCH is
529	#                                  still retried every cycle; only the line is rate-limited.

532	def _recorder_ram_blocked_line(now, notify_fn=None, free_fn=None):
533	    """NB45-A fault 1: the depth camera the 08:15 READY line names is held because free RAM is below the
534	    recorder floor. Say it ONCE (⏱, informational — never a 🙋; recorders are non-chain, §27d) with the
535	    MEASURED free RAM and the floor, so 'cameras down' at 09:09 is never silent again. Rate-limited via the
536	    watchdog state file; returns True if it said the line this call."""
537	    st = _read(STATE_FILE) or {}
538	    last = st.get("recorder_ram_line_ts")
539	    if last is not None and (now - last) / 60.0 < RECORDER_RAM_LINE_MIN:
540	        return False
541	    free = floor = None
542	    try:
543	        from scripts import memory_guard as MG
544	        free = (free_fn or MG.free_gb)()
545	        floor = MG.LOW_FREE_GB
546	    except Exception:
547	        pass
548	    (notify_fn or notify)("⏱ [E1] Camera (order-book depth) held — free RAM %s GB is below the %s GB "
549	                          "recorder floor; retrying every cycle, it starts the moment RAM frees."
550	                          % ("%.2f" % free if free is not None else "?",
551	                             "%.2f" % floor if floor is not None else "?"))
552	    st["recorder_ram_line_ts"] = now
553	    _write_state(st)
554	    return True

557	def resurrect_recorders(now, token_fn=None, ram_fn=None, running_fn=None, start_fn=None, log_fn=None,
558	                        notify_fn=None, free_fn=None):
559	    """§14.10 AMENDMENT (NB17/NB19-R item 58): relaunch any DEAD recorder lane — but ONLY when the Kite token is
560	    present AND free RAM is above the session floor; log each relaunch with the machine clock. Recorders only,
561	    never the stack, never a session. Reuses launch_e1.RECORDERS (the lanes) and launch_e1.start (single-instance,
562	    hidden). All gates injectable so the rule is unit-tested without touching the live box.
563	    NB45-A fault 1: with the token present but RAM below the floor, it no longer skips SILENTLY — it says one ⏱
564	    line naming the measured free RAM and keeps retrying every cycle (the camera the 08:15 line needs)."""
565	    token_fn = token_fn or _token_present
566	    ram_fn = ram_fn or _recorder_ram_ok
567	    if not token_fn():
568	        return {"relaunched": [], "reason": "no token (recorders not spun token-less)"}
569	    if not ram_fn():
570	        said = _recorder_ram_blocked_line(now, notify_fn, free_fn)
571	        return {"relaunched": [], "reason": "free RAM below the session floor", "line_said": said}
572	    from scripts import launch_e1
573	    start_fn = start_fn or launch_e1.start
574	    running_fn = running_fn or (lambda args: launch_e1.is_running(args, launch_e1.running_cmdlines()))
575	    relaunched = []
576	    for label, args in launch_e1.recorder_set():          # NB23: thin (depth only) until one clean trade
577	        if not running_fn(args):
578	            start_fn(label, args)                          # hidden, single-instance; logs to LAUNCH-RAM.log
579	            relaunched.append(label)
580	            if log_fn:
581	                log_fn("%s recorder relaunched: %s" % (_ist().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S IST"), label))
582	    return {"relaunched": relaunched}

585	def _conf_status_sig():
586	    """A fingerprint of the STATUS column of CONFORMANCE.md — the conforms/partial/missing/wall tokens in
587	    order. It changes only when a row's STATUS changes (R-E1-RING-FOREVER checklist watchdog)."""
588	    try:
589	        import hashlib
590	        import re as _re
